# /lib/systemd/system/multicast-relay.service

[Unit]
Description=Multicast Relay Service
After=network.target
Requires=network-online.target

[Service]

ExecStart=/usr/bin/python3 /usr/bin/multicast.py
Restart=always

user@host.v1.4:~$ sudo sed -n '1,260p' /usr/bin/multicast.py
import socket
import struct
import threading

# 需要转发的组播组和端口
GROUPS = [
    ("224.10.10.202", 6692),
    ("224.10.10.202", 7782),
    ("224.10.10.201", 6691),
    ("224.10.10.201", 7781),
]

# 接收组播的接口 (eth0)
RECV_IFACE = "10.21.33.106"
# 转发出去的接口 (eth1)
SEND_IFACE = "10.21.31.106"


def join_multicast_group(group, port, iface_ip):
    """创建并返回一个加入了组播组的 UDP socket"""
    sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM, socket.IPPROTO_UDP)
    sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    sock.bind((group, port))  # 绑定到组播端口

    # 加入组播组
    mreq = struct.pack("4s4s", socket.inet_aton(group), socket.inet_aton(iface_ip))
    sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_IP, socket.IP_ADD_MEMBERSHIP, mreq)

    return sock


def forwarder(group, port):
    """从 eth0 接收组播并转发到 eth1"""
    recv_sock = join_multicast_group(group, port, RECV_IFACE)

    send_sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM, socket.IPPROTO_UDP)
    # 指定发送接口为 eth1
    send_sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_IP, socket.IP_MULTICAST_IF, socket.inet_aton(SEND_IFACE))

    print(f"[Relay] Forwarding {group}:{port} from {RECV_IFACE} → {SEND_IFACE}")

    while True:
        data, addr = recv_sock.recvfrom(65535)
        send_sock.sendto(data, (group, port))


def main():
    threads = []
    for group, port in GROUPS:
        t = threading.Thread(target=forwarder, args=(group, port), daemon=True)
        t.start()
        threads.append(t)

    print("Multicast relay running...")
    for t in threads:
        t.join()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

>
> for group, port in groups:
>     s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM, socket.IPPROTO_UDP)
>     s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
>     try:
>         s.bind((group, port))
>         mreq = struct.pack("4s4s", socket.inet_aton(group), socket.inet_aton(iface))
>         s.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_IP, socket.IP_ADD_MEMBERSHIP, mreq)
>         print("OK  ", group, port)
>     except Exception as e:
>         print("FAIL", group, port, repr(e))
>     finally:
>         s.close()
> PY
OK   224.10.10.202 6692
OK   224.10.10.202 7782
OK   224.10.10.201 6691

关于IP修改：
原手册中该部分:配置参数文件  提供的下载的config.yaml内容如下：
# common:
#   msg_source: 1                         # 0: not use Lidar
#                                         # 1: packet message comes from online Lidar
#                                         # 2: packet message comes from ROS or ROS2
#                                         # 3: packet message comes from Pcap file
#   send_packet_ros: false                # true: Send packets through ROS or ROS2(Used to record packet)
#   send_point_cloud_ros: true            # true: Send point cloud through ROS or ROS2
# lidar:
#   - driver:
#       lidar_type: RSM1             #  LiDAR type - RS16, RS32, RSBP, RSAIRY, RSHELIOS, RSHELIOS_16P, RS128, RS80, RS48, RSP128, RSP80, RSP48, 
#                                    #               RSM1, RSM1_JUMBO, RSM2, RSM3, RSE1, RSMX.
                                   
#       msop_port: 6699              #  Msop port of lidar
#       difop_port: 7788             #  Difop port of lidar
#       imu_port: 0                  #  IMU port of lidar(only for RSAIRY, RSE1), 0 means no imu.
#                                    #  If you want to use IMU, please first set ENABLE_IMU_DATA_PARSE to ON in CMakeLists.txt 
#       user_layer_bytes: 0          #  Bytes of user layer. thers is no user layer if it is 0         
#       tail_layer_bytes: 0          #  Bytes of tail layer. thers is no tail layer if it is 0


#       min_distance: 0.2            #  Minimum distance of point cloud
#       max_distance: 200            #  Maximum distance of point cloud
#       use_lidar_clock: true        #  true--Use the lidar clock as the message timestamp
#                                    #  false-- Use the system clock as the timestamp
#       dense_points: false          #  true: discard NAN points; false: reserve NAN points
      
#       ts_first_point: true         #  true: time-stamp point cloud with the first point; false: with the last point;   
#                                    #  these parameters are used from mechanical lidar

#       start_angle: 0               #  Start angle of point cloud
#       end_angle: 360               #  End angle of point cloud

#                                    #  When msg_source is 3, the following parameters will be used
#       pcap_repeat: true            #  true: The pcap bag will repeat play   
#       pcap_rate: 1.0               #  Rate to read the pcap file
#       pcap_path: /home/robosense/lidar.pcap #The path of pcap file

#     ros:
#       ros_frame_id: rslidar                           #Frame id of packet message and point cloud message
#       ros_recv_packet_topic: /rslidar_packets          #Topic used to receive lidar packets from ROS
#       ros_send_packet_topic: /rslidar_packets          #Topic used to send lidar packets through ROS
#       ros_send_imu_data_topic: /rslidar_imu_data         #Topic used to send imu data through ROS
#       ros_send_point_cloud_topic: /rslidar_points      #Topic used to send point cloud through ROS
#       ros_queue_length: 100                            #Topic QoS history depth

# /lib/systemd/system/chrony.service
[Unit]
Description=chrony, an NTP client/server
Documentation=man:chronyd(8) man:chronyc(1) man:chrony.conf(5)
Conflicts=openntpd.service ntp.service ntpsec.service
Wants=time-sync.target
Before=time-sync.target
After=network.target

[Service]
Type=forking
PIDFile=/run/chronyd.pid
EnvironmentFile=-/etc/default/chrony
# Starter takes care of special cases mostly for containers
ExecStart=/usr/lib/systemd/scripts/chronyd-starter.sh $DAEMON_OPTS
ExecStartPost=-/usr/lib/chrony/chrony-helper update-daemon
PrivateTmp=yes
ProtectHome=yes
ProtectSystem=full

[Install]
Alias=chronyd.service
WantedBy=multi-user.target

# /run/systemd/generator.late/ptpd.service
# Automatically generated by systemd-sysv-generator

[Unit]
Documentation=man:systemd-sysv-generator(8)
SourcePath=/etc/init.d/ptpd
Description=LSB: start and stop ptpd
Before=multi-user.target
Before=multi-user.target
Before=multi-user.target
Before=graphical.target
After=network-online.target
After=remote-fs.target
Wants=network-online.target

[Service]
Type=forking
Restart=no
TimeoutSec=5min
IgnoreSIGPIPE=no
KillMode=process
GuessMainPID=no
RemainAfterExit=yes
SuccessExitStatus=5 6
ExecStart=/etc/init.d/ptpd start
ExecStop=/etc/init.d/ptpd stop

# /lib/systemd/system/ptp4l.service
[Unit]
Description=PTP (IEEE 1588) clock synchronization (ptp4l)
After=network.target
Requires=network-online.target

[Service]
Type=simple
ExecStart=/usr/sbin/ptp4l -H -E -4 -m -i eth0 -s
Restart=always
RestartSec=2

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target


# /lib/systemd/system/phc2sys.service

[Unit]
Description=Synchronize PTP hardware clock (PHC) to system clock
After=ptp4l.service
Requires=ptp4l.service

[Service]
Type=simple
ExecStart=/usr/sbin/phc2sys -m -s eth0 -c CLOCK_REALTIME -w  -O 0
Restart=always
RestartSec=2

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target

> from rclpy.qos import QoSProfile, ReliabilityPolicy, HistoryPolicy
stats = {}
>
> last = {}
> stats = {}
>
def ts(msg):
> def ts(msg):
>     return msg.header.stamp.sec + msg.header.stamp.nanosec * 1e-9
def check(name, t, gap_th):
>
> def check(name, t, gap_th):
>     s = stats.setdefault(name, {"n":0, "back":0, "gap":0, "maxdt":0.0})
>     if name in last:
>         dt = t - last[name]
            s["back"] += 1
>         s["maxdt"] = max(s["maxdt"], dt)
>         if dt < 0:
        if dt > gap_th:
>             s["back"] += 1
>             print(f"[BACK] {name}: dt={dt:.9f}, t={t:.9f}, last={last[name]:.9f}")
>         if dt > gap_th:
>             s["gap"] += 1
>             print(f"[GAP ] {name}: dt={dt:.6f}, t={t:.9f}")
>     last[name] = t
>     s["n"] += 1
qos = QoSProfile(depth=200, history=HistoryPolicy.KEEP_LAST,
>
> rclpy.init()
> node = rclpy.create_node("stamp_probe")
> qos = QoSProfile(depth=200, history=HistoryPolicy.KEEP_LAST,
>                  reliability=ReliabilityPolicy.BEST_EFFORT)
>
> node.create_subscription(Imu, "/IMU", lambda m: check("/IMU", ts(m), 0.1), qos)
> node.create_subscription(PointCloud2, "/LIDAR/POINTS", lambda m: check("/LIDAR/POINTS", ts(m), 0.15), qos)
>
> end = time.time() + 60
> while rclpy.ok() and time.time() < end:
>     rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=0.1)
>
> print(stats)
> rclpy.shutdown()

第一段：
m20@M20piper:~$ source /opt/ros/foxy/setup.bash
[ -f ~/rslidar_ws/install/setup.bash ] && source ~/rslidar_ws/install/setup.bash

OUT=~/m20_topic_audit_$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S).txt
echo "日志文件: $OUT"

{
  echo "================ BASIC ================"
  date '+%s.%N %F %T %Z %z'
  hostname
  whoami
  ip -br addr
  ip route
  echo

  echo "================ ROS ENV ================"
  echo "ROS_DOMAIN_ID=$ROS_DOMAIN_ID"
  echo "RMW_IMPLEMENTATION=$RMW_IMPLEMENTATION"
  printenv | egrep 'ROS|RMW|FAST|CYCLONE|DDS' || true
  echo

  echo "================ TOPIC LIST ================"
  ros2 topic list | sort
  echo

  echo "================ TOPIC TYPE + INFO ================"
  for t in $(ros2 topic list | sort); do
    echo
    echo "===== $t ====="
    ros2 topic type "$t" || true
    ros2 topic info "$t" || true
    ros2 topic info -v "$t" 2>/dev/null | sed -n '1,120p' || true
  done
} | tee "$OUT"m20@M20piper:~$ [ -f ~/robodog_nav_system/install/setup.bash ] && source ~/robodog_nav_system/install/setup.bash

echo "追加到: $OUT"

{
  echo
  echo "================ HZ IMPORTANT TOPICS ================"

  for t in \
    /IMU \
    /ODOM \
    /tf \
    /tf_static \
    /BATTERY_DATA \
    /JOINTS_DATA \
    /JOINTS_DATA_10HZ \
    /MOTION_INFO \
    /MOTION_STATE \
    /MOTION_STATUS \
    /NAV_STATUS \
    /PLANNER_STATUS \
    /GLOBAL_PLANNER_STATUS \
    /FAULT_STATUS \
    /EXCEPTION_NOTIFICATION \
    /LOCATION_STATUS \
    /LOCATION_STATUS/MATCHING_ERROR \
    /LIDAR/STATUS \
    /LIDAR/IMU201 \
    /LIDAR/IMU202 \
    /rslidar_points \
    /rslidar_points_front \
    /rslidar_points_rear \
    /accumulate_cloud/status_code \
    /passable_status_code
  do
    echo
    echo "===== HZ $t ====="
    ros2 topic type "$t" || true
    timeout 8 ros2 topic hz "$t" || true
  done
} | tee -a "$OUT"m20@M20piper:~$ [ -f ~/robodog_nav_system/install/setup.bash ] && source ~/robodog_nav_system/install/setup.bash

    '/impassable_area',

grid_topics = ['/traversal_cost']

class Probe(Node):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__('m20_backpack_topic_probe')
        self.seen = set()

        for t in pc_topics:
            self.create_subscription(PointCloud2, t, lambda m, t=t: self.pc(t, m), 10)

        for t in grid_topics:
            self.create_subscription(OccupancyGrid, t, lambda m, t=t: self.grid(t, m), 10)

        self.create_subscription(Imu, '/IMU', lambda m: self.once('/IMU', f'frame={m.header.frame_id} stamp={m.header.stamp.sec}.{m.header.stamp.nanosec:09d}'), 10)
        self.create_subscription(Odometry, '/ODOM', self.odom, 10)
        self.create_subscription(TFMessage, '/tf', self.tf, 10)

    def once(self, topic, text):
        if topic not in self.seen:
            self.seen.add(topic)
            print(f'[{topic}] {text}', flush=True)

    def pc(self, topic, msg):
        fields = ','.join(f.name for f in msg.fields)
        self.once(
            topic,
            f'frame={msg.header.frame_id} stamp={msg.header.stamp.sec}.{msg.header.stamp.nanosec:09d} '
            f'size={msg.width}x{msg.height} point_step={msg.point_step} fields={fields} dense={msg.is_dense}'
        )

    def grid(self, topic, msg):
        info = msg.info
        self.once(
            topic,
            f'frame={msg.header.frame_id} size={info.width}x{info.height} res={info.resolution} '
            f'origin=({info.origin.position.x:.3f},{info.origin.position.y:.3f}) data_len={len(msg.data)}'
        )

    def odom(self, msg):
        p = msg.pose.pose.position
        self.once('/ODOM', f'frame={msg.header.frame_id} child={msg.child_frame_id} pos=({p.x:.3f},{p.y:.3f},{p.z:.3f})')

    def tf(self, msg):
        for tr in msg.transforms:
            key = f'/tf {tr.header.frame_id}->{tr.child_frame_id}'
            if key not in self.seen:
                self.seen.add(key)
                print(f'[{key}]', flush=True)

rclpy.init()
node = Probe()
end = time.time() + 15
while time.time() < end and rclpy.ok():
    rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=0.2)
node.destroy_node()
rclpy.shutdown()

m20@M20piper:~/robodog_nav_system$ python3 - <<'PY'
> import time
> import rclpy
> from rclpy.node import Node
> from sensor_msgs.msg import PointCloud2
>
> topics = ['/rslidar_points_front', '/rslidar_points_rear']
>
> class Probe(Node):
>     def __init__(self):
>         super().__init__('rslidar_rate_probe')
>         self.count = {t: 0 for t in topics}
>         self.last = {}
>         for t in topics:
>             self.create_subscription(PointCloud2, t, lambda msg, t=t: self.cb(t, msg), 10)
>
>     def cb(self, topic, msg):
>         self.count[topic] += 1
>         self.last[topic] = (
>             msg.header.frame_id,
>             msg.header.stamp.sec,
>             msg.header.stamp.nanosec,
>             msg.width,
>             msg.height,
>             [f.name for f in msg.fields],
>         )
>
> rclpy.init()
> node = Probe()
    rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=0.2)

> start = time.time()
> while time.time() - start < 12:
>     rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=0.2)
>
> dt = time.time() - start
> for t in topics:
PY>     print(f'{t}: count={node.count[t]}, hz={node.count[t]/dt:.2f}, last={node.last.get(t)}')
>
> node.destroy_node()
> rclpy.shutdown()
> PY

m20@M20piper:~$ cat > ~/m20_ros_env.sh <<'EOF'
> #!/usr/bin/env bash
>
> _m20_drop_path_fragment() {
>   local var="$1"
>   local bad="$2"
>   local old="${!var}"
>   local new=""
>   local part
>
>   [ -z "$old" ] && return 0
>
>   IFS=':' read -ra parts <<< "$old"
>   for part in "${parts[@]}"; do
>     [ -z "$part" ] && continue
>     case "$part" in
>       *"$bad"*) continue ;;
>     esac
>     if [ -z "$new" ]; then
>       new="$part"
>     else
>       new="$new:$part"
>     fi
>   done
>
>   export "$var=$new"
> }
>
> for v in AMENT_PREFIX_PATH CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH COLCON_PREFIX_PATH PYTHONPATH LD_LIBRARY_PATH; do
>   _m20_drop_path_fragment "$v" "$HOME/rslidar_ws"
> done
>
> export ROS_DOMAIN_ID=0
> export RMW_IMPLEMENTATION=rmw_fastrtps_cpp
>
> [ -f /opt/ros/foxy/setup.bash ] && source /opt/ros/foxy/setup.bash
> [ -f "$HOME/robodog_nav_system/install/setup.bash" ] && source "$HOME/robodog_nav_system/install/setup.bash"
>
> for v in AMENT_PREFIX_PATH CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH COLCON_PREFIX_PATH PYTHONPATH LD_LIBRARY_PATH; do
>   _m20_drop_path_fragment "$v" "$HOME/rslidar_ws"
> done
>
> unset -f _m20_drop_path_fragment
> EOF

m20@M20piper:~/robodog_nav_system$ python3 - <<'PY'
> import time
> import rclpy
> from rclpy.node import Node
> from sensor_msgs.msg import Imu, PointCloud2
> from nav_msgs.msg import Odometry, OccupancyGrid
> from tf2_msgs.msg import TFMessage
>
> class Probe(Node):
>     def __init__(self):
>         super().__init__('m20_backpack_probe')
>         self.seen = set()
>
>         for t in ['/IMU', '/LIDAR/IMU201', '/LIDAR/IMU202']:
>             self.create_subscription(Imu, t, lambda m, t=t: self.imu_cb(t, m), 10)
>
>         for t in ['/ODOM', '/LIO_ODOM', '/LIO_ODOM_HIGH_FREQUENCY']:
>             self.create_subscription(Odometry, t, lambda m, t=t: self.odom_cb(t, m), 10)
>
>         for t in ['/rslidar_points_front', '/rslidar_points_rear', '/LIDAR/POINTS', '/LIDAR/POINTS2', '/LOC_BODY_POINTS']:
>             self.create_subscription(PointCloud2, t, lambda m, t=t: self.pc_cb(t, m), 10)
>
>         for t in ['/traversal_cost', '/GRID_MAP']:
>             self.create_subscription(OccupancyGrid, t, lambda m, t=t: self.grid_cb(t, m), 10)
>
>         self.create_subscription(TFMessage, '/tf', lambda m: self.tf_cb('/tf', m), 10)
>
>     def once(self, t, text):
>         if t not in self.seen:
>             self.seen.add(t)
>             print(f'[{t}] {text}', flush=True)
>
>     def imu_cb(self, t, m):
>         self.once(t, f'frame={m.header.frame_id} stamp={m.header.stamp.sec}.{m.header.stamp.nanosec:09d}')
>
>     def odom_cb(self, t, m):
>         p = m.pose.pose.position
>         self.once(t, f'frame={m.header.frame_id} child={m.child_frame_id} pos=({p.x:.3f},{p.y:.3f},{p.z:.3f})')
>
>     def pc_cb(self, t, m):
>         fields = ','.join(f.name for f in m.fields)
>         self.once(t, f'frame={m.header.frame_id} stamp={m.header.stamp.sec}.{m.header.stamp.nanosec:09d} size={m.width}x{m.height} fields={fields}')
>
>     def grid_cb(self, t, m):
>         info = m.info
>         self.once(t, f'frame={m.header.frame_id} size={info.width}x{info.height} res={info.resolution} data_len={len(m.data)}')
>
>     def tf_cb(self, t, m):
>         if m.transforms:
>             tr = m.transforms[0]
>             self.once(t, f'first_edge={tr.header.frame_id}->{tr.child_frame_id} count={len(m.transforms)}')
>
> rclpy.init()
> node = Probe()
> end = time.time() + 12
> while time.time() < end:
>     rclpy.spin_once(node, timeout_sec=0.2)
> print('seen:', sorted(node.seen))
> node.destroy_node()
> rclpy.shutdown()
> PY
[/IMU] frame= stamp=1784624216.299348491
[/LIDAR/IMU201] frame=lidar_link stamp=1784624216.300016896
[/LIDAR/IMU202] frame=lidar_link stamp=1784624216.301694976
[/ODOM] frame=map child= pos=(23909.194,-17928.844,-27121.570)
[/rslidar_points_front] frame=rslidar_front stamp=1784624216.200025320 size=1x15626 fields=x,y,z,intensity
[/rslidar_points_rear] frame=rslidar_rear stamp=1784624216.299990892 size=1x21096 fields=x,y,z,intensity
[/tf] first_edge=map->base_link count=1
seen: ['/IMU', '/LIDAR/IMU201', '/LIDAR/IMU202', '/ODOM', '/rslidar_points_front', '/rslidar_points_rear', '/tf']
